[EVENT: IRN21853]
Date: 2023-01-03
Country: Iran | Location: Tehran
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Iran) [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: BBC News; FARS News Agency (Iran); Iran International; Iranian Student News Agency; Islamic Republic News Agency; Jerusalem Post; Kurdistan24; Mehr News Agency; Tasnim News Agency; VOA; Young Journalists Club [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 January 2023, unidentified gunmen shot an IRGC commander in front of his house in Tehran city (Tehran) hitting him four times and killing him as a result. The IRGC commander in question oversaw Basij personnel in the city. One other person who was with the victim was injured and hospitalized. Some government sources claim the attack was a robbery attempt.
